HAN NAY RESTAURANT GROUP
April 12, 2021
Mayor & Members of the City Council
2101 O’NeiI Avenue
Cheyenne, WY 82001
Dear Mayor & Members of the Cheyenne City Council,
Please approve our Application for a Bar & Grill License for the Cheyenne, WY Dog Haus located at 3838
Atkin Street, Cheyenne, WY 92001.
HRARG DH Cheyenne WY, LLC, a wholly-owned subsidiary of HRA Restaurant Group, LLC, is excited to be
purchasing and operating the Dog Haus franchise in Cheyenne, WY.
The Dog Haus Franchise is known for its craft-casual gourmet hot dogs, sausages and burgers, premium
shakes and soft-serve ice cream, and a beer program that spotlights local brews. The Dog Haus mission
includes creating a successful corporation that pays forward to the community. Dog Haus has partnered
with No Kid Hungry, and a percentage of sales are donated to this organization.
We believe we have the vision, integrity and leadership to make this one of the most successful
restaurants in the Dog Haus family.
Thank you in advance for considering our Application.
Sincerely,
R. Craig Hannay

ies, Inc. as

TERMS AND CONDITIONS
ARTICLE 1. PREMISES
1.1
Identification of Premises.
In consideration of the rents, covenants, and agreements contained
herein, Landlord leases to Tenant and Tenant leases from Landlord that certain restaurant located at Lot 8, Block 1,
North College Drive, Cheyenne, Wyoming, as more particularly identified on Exhibit A
(the “Premises”). The
building in which the Premises are located (and constituting a portion ofthe Premises), with the street address set forth
on Exhibit A attached hereto, is sometimes referred to hereinafter as the “Building.”
1.2
e. The Premises estimate that the Building will consist of a floor area of approximately 2,828
rentable square feet as generally set forth on the site plan attached hereto as Exhibit B. The purpose of the attached
Exhibit B is to show the approximate location of the Premises in the Building only, and is not meant to constitute an
agreement as to the construction of the Premises, the location or elements thereof or the location of the access-ways
to the Premises or the Building.
ARTICLE 2. TERM AND USE
2.1
Rent Commencement Date and Term. The initial term (the “Initial Term”) of this Lease shall be
for a period of 20 Lease Years (defined below) beginning on the Rent Commencement Date and ending at midnight
on the last day of the last full Lease Year in the Term. For purposes of this Lease, “Rent Commencement Date”
means the date that is the earlier of(i) ten (10) days after issuance of a certificate of occupancy for the Premises, and
(ii) the date Tenant opens for business.
For purposes of this Lease, “Lease Month” means each consecutive one-
month period commencing on the Rent Commencement date, and a “Lease Year” means a period of 12 Lease Months.
Landlord shall use its commercially reasonable efforts to cause to achieve Substantial Completion of the
Improvements within 120 days of the date of the physical on-site commencement of construction of the Improvements
at the Premises.
If Substantial Completion does not occur within 120 days of the date of the physical on-site
commencement of construction of the Improvements at the Premises due to any cause other than force majeure or
Tenant Delay, then Tenant shall receive a day-for-day credit of Base Rent for each day of delay.
If Substantial
Completion does not occur within 150 days of the date of the physical on-site commencement of the Improvements
at the Premises due to any cause other than force majeure or Tenant Delay, then Tenant shall receive a credit of Base
Rent equal to the number of days beyond 150 multiplied by 2 plus any prior accrued credit. As used herein, “Tenant
Delay” shall mean any delay in Substantial Completion caused by (i) Tenant’s changes in any drawings, plans or
specifications for the Improvements, (ii) Tenant’s or Tenant’s franchisors’ failure to promptly respond to information
and requests of Landlord, and (iii) any other act or omission of Tenant under this Lease.
2.2
Renewal Options. Provided Tenant is not in default of any material term, condition or covenant
contained in this Lease, Tenant shall have the option to extend the Initial Term for three (3) additional renewal terms
of five (5) Lease Years each (each, a “Renewal Term,” and together with the Initial Term, the “Term” or the “term”)
on the same terms and conditions as provided herein. Notice of the exercise of such option to renew shall be given by
Tenant to Landlord, in writing, no later than 180 days before the expiration of the then existing term. The written
notice to Landlord shall unequivocally state that the Lease has been extended and shall be given in accordance with
the provisions of Article 27 of this Lease.
2.3
Use.
The Premises are leased to Tenant and Tenant shall only use the Premises for the operation of a
Dog Haus restaurant selling hot dogs, sausages, hamburgers, chicken, alcoholic or malt beverages, and other food and drinks
typical of a Dog Haus restaurant. Tenant shall not use the Premises for any purposes other than those enumerated in the
preceding sentence without the prior written consent of Landlord, not to be unreasonably withheld, conditioned or delayed.
Tenant will keep the Premises in a clean and orderly condition, free ofobjectionable noise, odors or nuisances.
ARTICLE 3. RENT; ADDITIONAL RENT AND IMPOSITIONS
3.1
General. The Base Rent, as defined below, and all Additional Rent, as also defmed below (Base
Rent and Additional Rent are sometimes collectively referred to herein as “Rent”) shall be payable commencing on
the Rent Commencement Date. If the Base Rent shall be determined for a period less than a full Lease Month, then
5

the Base Rent for such period shall be prorated accordingly. All payments of Rent and other sums shall be made in
lawful monies of the United States and paid or mailed to the address for Landlord in accordance with the provisions
of Article 27 of this Lease or my wire transfer as agreed upon in writing by the Landlord and Tenant. Landlord may
designate, in writing any other payee or address to which Tenant shall pay the Base Rent, in its sole discretion. Rent
due during each Lease Year shall be payable without demand, set-off or deduction, in equal monthly installments, and
the initial payment of Rent shall be due and payable on or before the Rent Commencement Date, and each payment
of Rent thereafter shall be due and payable on the first of each Lease Month thereafter.
3.2
Base Rent. Tenant shall pay to Landlord, for the use and occupancy of the Premises, at the times
and in the manner provided in this Lease, the following amounts. The base rent (the “Base Rent”) for the first Lease
Year will be $175,000.00. Beginning on the fifth (Sth) annual anniversary of the Rent Commencement Date and on
each subsequent fifth (sth) annual anniversary thereafter during the Lease Term, the Base Rent shall increase by ten
percent (10%) of the Base Rent during the prior 5-year period.
Landlord and Tenant shall enter into a rent
commencement certificate in the form attached hereto as Exhibit C attached hereto (the “Rent Commencement
Certificate”).
3.3
Additional Rent; Impositions; Utilities.
a.
Additional Rent. In addition to Base Rent, Tenant shall pay to Landlord, for the use and
occupancy of the Premises, all additional amounts required to be paid by Tenant to Landlord hereunder, whether
designed as “Additional Rent” hereunder or not. All amounts payable by Tenant to Landlord hereunder are referred
to herein as “Additional Rent.” Except as otherwise described herein, Additional Rent shall be payable the same time
as the payment of Base Rent.
b.
Impositions.
i.
During the Term of this Lease, Tenant shall pay or shall cause to be paid all
Impositions directly to the governmental authority charged with the collection thereof. Each Imposition, or installment
thereof during the Term shall be paid not later than seven (7) days prior to the due date thereof. However, if, by law,
any Imposition may at the option of the taxpayer be paid in installments (whether or not interest shall accrue on the
unpaid balance of such Imposition), Tenant may exercise the option to pay the same in such installments and shall be
responsible for the payment of such installments only, together with applicable interest, if any, provided that all such
installment payments together with applicable interest, if any, relating to periods prior to the expiration of the Term
shall be made prior to the expiration of the Term. Tenant shall promptly notifS’ Landlord if Tenant shall have elected
to pay any such Imposition in installments.
ii.
Tenant shall, within seven (7) days following each due date, furnish to Landlord
official receipts of the appropriate governmental authority, or other evidence reasonably satisfactory to Landlord,
evidencing the payment of such Impositions.
iii.
Tenant shall not be required to pay municipal, state or federal income, gross
receipts, inheritance, estate, succession, profit, capital or transfer gains taxes of Landlord, or any corporate franchise
tax imposed upon Landlord or any transfer or gains tax imposed on Landlord.
iv.
Any Imposition relating to a period, a part of which is included within the Term
and a part of which is included in a period of time before the Rent Commencement Date or after the expiration of the
Term (whether or not such Imposition shall be assessed, levied, confirmed, imposed upon or in respect of or become
a lien upon the Premises, or shall become payable, during the Term), shall be apportioned between Landlord and
Tenant as of the Rent Commencement Date or expiration of the Term, as the case may be, so that Tenant shall pay
only that portion of such Imposition which that part of such fiscal period included in the period of time after the Rent
Commencement Date or before the expiration of the Term bears to such fiscal period, and Landlord shall pay the
remainder thereof. Other than in respect of Impositions relating, in part, to a period of time before the Commencement
Date, no such apportionment of Impositions shall be made if this Lease is terminated as the result of an Event of
Default.
6

c.
Utilities. Notwithstanding the payment of Additional Rent, Tenant is also solely obligated
to pay for Tenant’s use of utilities regarding the Premises, which Landlord shall ensure shall be separately metered,
during the Term (as more particularly set forth in Article 14 below). If requested by Landlord, Tenant will pay such
utilities directly to the applicable governmental authority or directly to the applicable utility. Tenant shall pay all
utility fees, including but not limited to hook-up.
3.4
Fee Mortgage. If the Premises are encumbered by a Fee Mortgage, the following shall apply: (a) if
any Fee Mortgagee requires that amounts (estimated and/or actual) necessary to pay any Impositions and/or insurance
premiums be deposited with it in advance (which right, if any, shall be subject to the rights of a Leasehold Mortgagee),
Tenant, upon at least seven (7) days’ prior written notice from Landlord, shall pay the amounts so required to the Fee
Mortgagee in accordance with the requirements of such Fee Mortgagee and such payments shall constitute full
satisfaction of Tenant’s obligations hereunder with respect to the amounts paid to the Fee Mortgagee; (b) if any
Imposition for which such payment is timely made to the Fee Mortgagee is not paid when due, Landlord (and not
Tenant) shall take such steps as are necessary to cause the same to be paid, unless Tenant has been instructed in writing
by Landlord to pay such Imposition directly to the Fee Mortgagee; and (c) if any Imposition for which such payment
has been made to the Fee Mortgagee becomes delinquent, Tenant may, but is not obligated to, pay the same and deduct
the amount of such payment, plus any interest and penalties and any other reasonable expenses of Tenant incurred in
connection therewith, from the next instalhnent or installments of Rent.
3.5
Absolute Net Lease. Landlord and Tenant agree and hereby acknowledge that this Lease is intended
to be an absolute net lease, meaning that once Landlord has performed its obligations relative to construction of the
Improvements and all costs ofImpositions, costs incurred for repairing, maintaining and insuring the Premises hereunder,
and the costs ofkeeping the Premises and Improvements in compliance will all laws, regulations and covenants applicable
thereto, including without limitation payment of taxes, assessments, and any other charges related to or imposed on the
Premises by a governmental or other valid authority with jurisdiction over the Premises, shall be the sole and exclusive
obligation of Tenant. Unless caused by the negligence or misconduct of Landlord or its agents, for the avoidance of
doubt, Landlord shall have no obligation to the repair, maintain, or replace any improvement, including the Building, on
or in the Premises, except insofar as such repairs, maintenance or replacement are covered by the warranties provided by
contractors, manufacturers, and suppliers, in which case Landlord shall assign, without recourse and to the extent
allowable by law, such warranties to Tenant. Tenant shall be responsible for all repairs, maintenance and replacements
necessary to keep the Premises in good condition, repair and legally/administratively compliant, including, without
limitation, the slab, foundation, structural walls, roof structure and covering and all HVAC equipment.
3.6
Certain Definitions. The following terms shall have the respective meanings ascribed thereto:
“Impositions” shall mean any and all: (a) property taxes of every kind and nature (including any
school, county, city, town or other taxes); (b) real property assessments (whether general, special, business
improvement district or otherwise); (c) personal property taxes; (d) occupancy and rent taxes; (e) water, water
meter and sewer rents, rates and charges; (f) excises; (g) levies; (h) license and permit fees; (i) service charges
with respect to police protection, fire protection, street and highway construction, maintenance and lighting,
sanitation and water supply, if any; (j) fmes, penalties and other similar or like governmental charges
applicable to the foregoing and any interest or costs with respect thereto; and (k) any and all other
governmental levies, fees, rents, assessments or taxes and charges, general and special, ordinary and
extraordinary, foreseen and unforeseen, of any kind and nature whatsoever, and any interest or costs with
respect thereto, which at any time during the Term are, or, if the Premises or any part thereof or the owner
thereof were not exempt therefrom, would have been: (i) assessed, levied, confirmed, imposed upon or would
have become due and payable out ofor in respect of, or would have been charged with respect to, the Premises
or any document to which Tenant is a party creating or transferring an interest or estate in the Premises
(excluding any capital gains taxes imposed in connection with the execution of this Lease), or the use and
occupancy thereof by Tenant; or (ii) encumbrances or liens on: (A) the Premises; or (B) the sidewalks or
streets in front of or adjoining the Premises; or (C) any vault (other than a vault in respect of which a utility
company is obligated to pay any charge specified above or which is exempt from any such charge by reason
of use thereof by any such utility company), passageway or space in, over or under such sidewalk or street;
or (D) any other appurtenances of the Premises; or (E) any personalty used in the operation thereof or (F)
the Rent (or any portion thereof).
7

“Fee Mortgage” shall mean any fmancing obtained by Landlord, as evidenced by any mortgage,
deed of trust, assignment of leases and rents, financing statement or other instruments, and secured by the
interest of Landlord in the Premises, including any extensions, modifications, amendments, replacements,
supplements, renewals, refinancings and consolidations thereof.
“Fee Mortgagee” shall mean the holder of a Fee Mortgage.
ARTICLE 4. IMPROVEMENTS
Landlord agrees to provide tenant with certain tenant improvements (the “Improvements”), all as set forth
on Exhibit U hereto. The respective obligations of Landlord and Tenant with respect to the Improvements are set
forth on Exhibit D hereto.
Upon substantial completion of the Improvements and issuance of a certificate of
occupancy (“Substantial Completion”), Tenant shall conduct a walk-through inspection of the Premises with
Landlord or Landlord’s representative, and shall compile a list of items which remain to be completed (the “Punch
list”) which do not materially interfere with Tenant’s use of the Premises. Tenant will inspect and provide the Punch-
list to Landlord within seven (7) business days following the walk-through inspection. Landlord shall complete the
Punch-list items with commercially reasonable diligence not to exceed thirty (30) calendar days. Upon completion of
the Improvements and the Punch-list, Landlord shall assign all warranties to Tenant to which Landlord may have
rights applicable to the Premises or any portion thereof provided by any manufacturers, designers and constructors of
the Premises or any portion thereof. Landlord agrees to take such other actions as may be necessary to effectuate such
assignment of warranties to Tenant.
ARTICLES. SUBORDINATION, ESTOPPEL CERTIFICATE AND ATTORNMENT; MORTGAGES
5.1
Subordination and Attornment. Tenant shall, at the request of Landlord, execute a Subordination
and Attomment Agreement (“SAA”) in commercially reasonable form delivered to Tenant by Landlord, providing
that, among other things, Tenant will, in writing, subordinate its rights hereunder to the interest of any ground lessor
of the Building in which the Premises is situated and to the lien of the mortgage or deed of trust of any lender
encumbering the land and improvements of which the Premises is a part, and upon any improvements hereafter placed
upon the land of which the Premises is a part and to all advances made or to be made upon the security thereof.
Provided that the applicable lender executes the SAA referred to above, Tenant agrees and acknowledges that this
Lease is subordinate to the lien of any mortgage or deed of trust of such lender or ground lessor and if such lender or
ground lessor succeeds to the interests of Landlord under this Lease, then, at such lender’s or lessor’s election
(a) Tenant shall be bound to such lender under all ofthe terms, covenants and conditions of this Lease for the remaining
balance of the term hereof with the same force and effect as if such lender were the lessor hereunder, and Tenant does
hereby agree to attorn to such lender as its lessor without requiring the execution of any further instruments
immediately upon such lender succeeding to the interests of Landlord under this Lease. Tenant agrees to execute and
deliver to such lender any instrument reasonably requested by it to evidence such attomment; and (b) subject to the
observance and performance by Tenant of all the terms, covenants and conditions of this Lease on the part of Tenant
to be observed and performed, such lender shall recognize the leasehold estate of Tenant under all of the terms and
conditions of this Lease for the remaining balance of the term with the same force and effect as if such lender were
the lessor under this Lease. In the event any proceedings are brought for foreclosure, or in the event of the exercise
of the power of sale under any mortgage or deed of trust, upon any such foreclosure or sale, Tenant agrees to recognize
such beneficiary or purchaser as Landlord under this Lease, provided Tenant’s rights under this Lease continue
unabated.
5.2
Fee Mortgages. Landlord may mortgage its fee interest in the Premises.
5.3
Leasehold Mortgages.
a.
Tenant, and every permitted successor and assign of Tenant, shall have the right to
encumber its interest in this Lease, with Landlord’s prior written consent, not to be unreasonably withheld, conditioned
or delayed, under any one or more Leasehold Mortgages, provided, however, that all rights acquired under the
Leasehold Mortgage(s) shall be subject to each of the provisions set forth in this Lease and to all rights and interests
of the Landlord therein. Landlord further consents to any encumbrance of Tenant’s interest in this Lease resulting
8

from the refinancing of the indebtedness initially secured by the Approved Leasehold Mortgage (the “Refinanced
Approved Leasehold Mortgage”). If, from time to time, Tenant or Tenant’s permitted successors or assigns shall
encumber this Lease with a Leasehold Mortgage, and if the Leasehold Mortgagee delivers to Landlord an executed
counterpart of such Leasehold Mortgage, together with each assignment thereof certified by the holder of the
Leasehold Mortgage to be true together with written notice specif’ing the name and address of the Leasehold
Mortgagee and the pertinent recording data with respect to the Leasehold Mortgage, Landlord agrees that, anything in
this Lease to the contrary notwithstanding, from and after the date of receipt by Landlord of such notice and for the
duration of such Leasehold Mortgage, the provisions of this Article 5 shall apply.
b.
Landlord agrees that in the event of a termination of this Lease by reason of any default by
Tenant, or if Tenant rejects the Lease in a bankruptcy proceeding, and subject to the rights herein granted to Leasehold
Mortgagee, Landlord will enter into a lease (the “Mortgagee Lease”) of the Premises with the Leasehold Mortgagee
for the remainder of the Term effective as of the date of termination, at the same Rent and upon the same terms,
provisions, covenants and agreements as contained in this Lease, provided:
i.
Leasehold Mortgagee shall make written request upon Landlord for the execution
of such a Mortgagee Lease within five (5) days after the date of termination and shall, within seven (7) days after its
receipt from Landlord of a written statement of all sums then due to Landlord under this Lease, pay to Landlord all
such sums.
ii.
Leasehold Mortgagee shall pay to Landlord at the time of the execution and
delivery of the Mortgagee Lease any sums that at the time of its execution and delivery would be due pursuant to this
Lease but for the termination, and in addition, all reasonable attorney’s fees and expenses which Landlord shall have
actually incurred.
iii.
Leasehold Mortgagee shall perform and observe all covenants contained in the
Mortgagee Lease on Tenant’s part to be performed during such period of time commencing with the date of the
execution of the Mortgagee Lease and terminating upon the expiration or earlier termination of the Mortgagee Lease
or the abandonment or surrender of possession of the Premises under the Mortgagee Lease and shall further remedy
any other conditions that Tenant was obligated to perform under the terms of this Lease.
iv.
Leasehold Mortgagee, as Tenant under the Mortgagee Lease, shall have the same
right, title and interest in and to the Premises and the right to use the Improvements thereon as Tenant had under this
Lease.
ARTICLE 6. CONDITION OF PREMISES
Tenant accepts the Premises in its present “AS IS” condition, without warranty or representation of Landlord
of any kind or nature whatsoever, express or implied, except as stated herein. Tenant represents and warrants that it
has inspected the Premises prior to the execution of this Lease and that the condition and construction of the Premises
is acceptable to Tenant hereunder.
ARTICLE 7. REPAIRS AND MAINTENANCE
Except as otherwise specifically set forth herein, Tenant shall, at its sole cost and expense, maintain the entire
Premises, including, but not limited to, the Restaurant and any other improvements now or hereafter existing therein
or thereon, in good condition and at all times to the conditions required and/or prescribed by Landlord. Tenant’s
obligation to maintain and repair includes specifically, but is not to be limited to, the maintenance and repair and/or
replacement of the following: fixtures, facilities or equipment contained inside the Premises, the heating, air-
conditioning, electrical and plumbing systems inside the Premises; exterior and interior doors; roof, windows and
glass; parking area, landscaped areas and any signs or other equipment installed and used by Tenant. Tenant shall
make such replacements of any of the foregoing as may become reasonably necessary during the term of this Lease
or any extension thereof. The provisions of this Article 7 shall not apply in the case of damage or destruction by fire
or other casualty within two (2) years of the end of the initial term of this Lease, or any extension thereof or in the
case of eminent domain, in which events the obligations of Tenant shall be controlled by either Article 16 or Article
25 hereof, as the case may be. In carrying out its obligations as set forth above in this Article 7, Tenant agrees to
9

conform to all requirements of law, the regulations of applicable public authorities, and the requirements of
insurers. Further, Tenant shall not take any action nor permit any action to be taken which would result in or cause
the loss, termination or forfeiture of any easement right appurtenant to the Premises or which would result in the
violation of any covenants, conditions or restrictions burdening the Premises
Tenant shall also be responsible to pay all janitorial and cleaning costs to the keep the Premises clean and
free of debris. If repairs for which the Tenant is responsible are not completed with ten (10) days after Tenant has
received written notice from Landlord of such state of disrepair or if such repairs cannot reasonably be completed
within such ten (10) day period and Tenant shall fail to commence such repairs within ten (10) days after notice and
proceed diligently thereafter, then Landlord may perform such repairs and charge the actual cost thereof to Tenant as
Additional Rent. Notwithstanding anything herein to the contrary, Landlord shall be responsible for all maintenance
and repairs necessary as a result of the gross negligence or willful misconduct of Landlord, its employees, agents,
contractors and representatives.
ARTICLE 8. ENVIRONMENTAL MATTERS
8.1
Warranties of Tenant and Indemnification of Landlord.
For purposes of this Lease, the term
“Hazardous Materials” shall mean any hazardous or toxic substance or any substance which is or becomes regulated
by any governmental authority, including without limitation any substance which is (a) defined as a “Hazardous
Substance,” “Hazardous Waste,” or “Extremely Hazardous Substance” pursuant to any provision of the United States
Code, including United States Code sections commonly known as the Federal Water Pollution Control Act, the Federal
Resource Conservation and Recovery Act, the Comprehensive Environmental Response Compensation and Liability
Act, and the Superfund Amendments and Reauthorizations Act of 1986, (b) defmed as
a hazardous substance or
material pursuant to any state or local law, ordinance or regulation governing the Premises (“Hazardous Material
Law”), (c) petroleum or a petroleum by-product, (d) asbestos or asbestos containing material, (e) a pesticide, (f) a
polychlorinated biphenyl, or (g) or any other substance known to cause cancer or other illnesses.
Tenant shall
indemnify, defend and hold Landlord and its officers, managers, members, shareholders, employees, agents,
successors and assigns harmless from, and Landlord shall have no liability for or responsibility to pay, any claims,
judgments, damages, fines, penalties, costs, liabilities (including sums paid in settlement of claims) or loss including
attorney’s fees, consultant’s fees, and expert fees which arise during or after the Term in connection with the presence
of Hazardous Materials in, on or under the Building, including, but limited to the Premises, related in any way, in
whole or in part, to the activities ofTenant, unless and to the extent the presence of such Hazardous Materials is caused
by Landlord, its officers, employees or agents. Also,
a.
Tenant warrants that it shall not cause any Hazardous Materials to be used, generated,
stored or disposed of on, under or about, or transported to or from the Premises or the Building, unless the
same is specifically approved in advance by Landlord, in writing, other than small quantities of retail,
household and office chemicals customarily sold over-the-counter to the public and which are related to
Tenant’s permitted uses of the Premises and which are used and disposed of in compliance with all Hazardous
Material Law or other applicable law;
b.
Tenant warrants that it shall comply with all obligations imposed by Hazardous Material
Law, any other applicable law, environmental laws and all other restrictions and regulations upon the use,
generation, storage or disposal of Hazardous Materials at, to or from the Premises;
c.
Tenant warrants that it shall deliver promptly to Landlord true and correct copies of all
notices received by Tenant from any governmental authority with respect to the use, generation, storage or
disposal by Tenant of Hazardous Materials at, to or from the Premises or Building and shall immediately
notify Landlord both by telephone and in writing of any unauthorized discharge of Hazardous Materials by
Tenant that Tenant reasonably believes poses an imminent hazard to the Premises, the Building, the public
or the environment;
d.
Tenant shall complete fully, truthfully and promptly any questionnaires sent by Landlord
with respect to Tenant’s use of the Premises and its use, generation, storage and disposal of Hazardous
Materials at, to or from the Premises, which are reasonably required;
10

e.
If Landlord conducts any environmental inspections because it has reasonable cause to
believe that Tenant’s activities have or are likely to result in a violation of Hazardous Material Law or any
other applicable law or a release of Hazardous Materials on the Premises or the Building and such violation
by Tenant has actually occurred, then Tenant shall pay to Landlord, as Additional Rent, the costs incurred by
Landlord for such inspections;
f.
Tenant shall cease immediately upon written notice from Landlord any activity which
violates any Hazardous Material Law or any other applicable law; and
g.
After notice to and approval by Landlord, which approval shall not be unreasonably
withheld, Tenant shall promptly remove, clean up, dispose of or otherwise remediate, in accordance with
Hazardous Material Law, or any other applicable law and good commercial practice, any Hazardous
Materials on, under or about the Premises resulting from Tenant’s acts on the Premises.
The foregoing indemnifications ofTenant shall survive any assignment, transfer or termination of this Lease.
8.2
Warranties of Landlord and Indemnification of Tenant. Landlord represents and warrants to Tenant
that, to its knowledge, there does not exist on the Premises or the land upon which the Building is located any
Hazardous Materials except as stated in any report provided by Landlord to Tenant. Landlord shall indemnify, defend
and hold Tenant and its officers, managers, members, shareholders, employees and agents harmless from, and Tenant
shall have no liability for or responsibility to pay, any claims, judgments, damages, fines, penalties, costs, liabilities
(including sums paid in settlement of claims) or loss including attorney’s fees, consultant’s fees, and expert fees which
arise from the use or release of Hazardous Materials on the Premises before Tenant’s occupancy of the Premises,
except to the extent such use or release was caused by the activities of Tenant or its employees or invitees.
ARTICLE 9. ALTERATIONS TO PREMISES
Tenant shall not make any material exterior or structural alterations in any portion of the Premises, nor any
alterations in the storefront or the exterior of the Premises without, in each instance, first obtaining the express written
consent of Landlord, which may be withheld in Landlord’s sole and absolute discretion. All alterations, additions and
improvements made by Tenant to or upon the Premises, except for Tenant’s Property (defined below), shall at once
when made or installed or be deemed to have been attached to the Premises and to have become the property of
Landlord; provided, however, if prior to termination of this Lease, or thirty (30) days thereafter, Landlord so directs
by written notice to Tenant, Tenant shall promptly remove the additions, improvements, or fixtures and shall repair
any damage occasioned by removal of additions, improvements, or fixtures and in default thereof Landlord may effect
said removal and repairs at Tenant’s sole expense.
ARTICLE 10. FIXTURES AND PERSONAL PROPERTY
Any trade fixtures, business equipment, inventory, trademarked items, signs, counters, shelving, showcases,
mirrors and other personal property installed in or on the Premises by Tenant at its expense and which may be removed
without material damage to the Premises (collectively “Tenant’s Property”), shall remain the property of Tenant.
Landlord agrees that Tenant shall have the right, at any time or from time to time, to remove any and all of Tenant’s
Property. Tenant at its expense shall immediately repair any damage occasioned by the removal of Tenant’s Property
and upon expiration or earlier termination of this Lease, shall leave the Premises in a neat and clean condition, free of
debris, normal wear and tear expected. Notwithstanding Tenant’s payment ofAdditional Rent, Tenant shall pay before
delinquency all taxes, assessments, license fees and public charges levied, assessed or imposed upon its business
operation in the Premises as well as upon Tenant’s Property, or upon the Premises. If any such items of property are
assessed with property of Landlord, then such assessment shall be equitably divided between Landlord and Tenant to
the end that Tenant shall pay only its equitable portion of such assessment. Landlord shall reasonably determine the
basis of so prorating any such assessments and such determination shall be binding upon both Landlord and Tenant.
11

ARTICLE 11. SIGNAGE
Tenant may place (i) permanent signage referring to its operating business conducted on or about the
Premises, and (ii) such temporary advertising or other signage as is in compliance with the applicable laws, regulations
and/or ordinances of applicable governmental authorities and the reasonable signage rules and regulations
promulgated by Landlord from time to time. All signage shall be at the sole cost and expense of Tenant, and Tenant
shall maintain all signage in good condition and repair. Notwithstanding the preceding, in no circumstance shall
Tenant place any balloon or similar temporary promotional advertisements on or about the exterior of the Premises
without Landlord’s prior written consent, not to be unreasonably withheld, conditioned or delayed.
ARTICLE 12. LIENS
Tenant shall not permit to be created nor to remain undischarged any lien, encumbrance or charge arising out
of any work or work claim of any contractor, mechanic, laborer of Tenant or material supplied by a materialman to
Tenant which might be, or become, a lien or encumbrance or charge upon the Premises or the Building. If any lien or
notice of lien on account of an alleged debt of Tenant or any notice of contract by a party engaged by Tenant or
Tenant’s contractor to work in the Premises shall be claimed against the Premises or the Building, Tenant shall, within
thirty (30) days after notice of the claim, cause the same to be discharged of record by payment, deposit or bond.
Tenant’s failure to do so shall constitute a default under this Lease and Landlord shall have the remedies available in
Article 21 of this Lease.
ARTICLE 13. LAWS AND ORDINANCES
13.1
Compliance. Tenant agrees to comply with all laws, ordinances, orders and regulations regarding
Tenant’s use, occupancy or alteration of the Premises, or the cleanliness, safety or operation thereof. Tenant agrees
to comply with the reasonable regulations, requirements, or recommendations with respect to the use, occupancy or
alteration of the Premises or the cleanliness, safety or operation thereof made or issued by any insurance underwriter,
inspection bureau or similar agency that may affect the insurance coverage for the Premises, provided such regulations,
requirements, or recommendations do not significantly and unreasonably affect Tenant’s use of the Premises. Tenant
also agrees to permit Landlord to comply with such regulations, recommendations requirements with respect to any
portion of the Premises maintained by Landlord.
13.2
Illegal and Improper Use; Nuisance. Tenant agrees not to (i) permit any illegal practice to be carried
on or committed on the Premises; (ii) make use of or allow the Premises to be used for any purpose that might
invalidate or increase the rate of insurance therefor; (iii) keep or use or permit to be kept or used on the Premises any
flammable fluids, gases, or explosives without the prior written permission of Landlord except for normal cleaning
products; (iv) use the Premises for any purpose whatsoever which might create a nuisance; (v) deface or injure the
Premises or the Building; (vi) overload the floor; (vii) commit or suffer any waste; or (viii) install any electrical
equipment that overloads lines.
13.3
Electrical Equipment. In connection with any electrical equipment on the Premises, Tenant shall,
at Tenant’s own expense, make from time to time whatever changes are reasonably necessary to comply with the
requirements of the insurance inspectors, underwriters, government authorities and codes.
13.4
ADA.
Tenant shall comply with the Americans with Disabilities Act of 1990 (“ADA”) and the
regulations promulgated thereunder. Tenant hereby expressly assumes all responsibility for compliance with the ADA
relating to the interior, non-structural portions of the Premises and Tenant’s specific use of the Premises.
Any
alterations to the interior, non-structural portions of the Premises made by Tenant for the purpose of complying with
the ADA or which otherwise require compliance with the ADA shall be done in accordance with the provisions of
this Lease; provided, that the Landlord’s consent to such alterations shall not constitute either Landlord’s assumption,
in whole or in part, of Tenant’s responsibility for compliance with the ADA, or representation or confirmation by
Landlord that such alterations comply with the provisions of the ADA.
Tenant shall be responsible for the costs and expenses arising from or relating to ADA compliance of the
exterior of the building in which the Premises are located including, but not limited to, the parking areas, walks,
signage and access to the Premises.
12

ARTICLE 14. SERVICES
14.1
Payment. Tenant shall be solely responsible for and shall promptly pay all charges for the use and
consumption of sewer, gas, electricity, water, phone and all other utility services used within the Premises, including
all connection fees and meter installation costs for said utility services.
14.2
Garbage Collection. Tenant shall obtain and pay for its own service for the collection and disposal
of refuse and garbage.
14.3
Interruption of Services. Landlord shall not be liable to Tenant in damages or otherwise if the said
utilities or services are interrupted or terminated because of necessary repairs, installations, improvements, problems
outside the Building, or any cause beyond Landlord’s reasonable control, and not directly or indirectly attributable to
the negligence of or breach of this Lease by Landlord or its agents, nor shall any such interruption or termination of
utility service relieve Tenant of the performance of any of its obligations hereunder unless directly or indirectly
attributable to the negligence of breach of this Lease by Landlord or its agents.
14.4
Additional Utility Facilities.
If any equipment installed by Tenant requires additional utility
facilities the same shall be installed at Tenant’s expense in compliance with all code requirements and plans and
specifications, which must be approved in writing by Landlord.
14.5
Landlord’s Responsibility. Landlord shall ensure that all utility connections, meters and services
applicable to the Premises are installed prior to Tenant’s occupancy of the Premises.
ARTICLE 15. PARKING AND ACCESS
Tenant shall be provided with the right to use parking areas and driveways, and means of ingress and egress
to the Building, sufficient to satisfy governmental and ADA requirements. Tenant shall have access to all parking
spaces located on the Premises.
ARTICLE 16. DAMAGE TO PREMISES
In the event that the Premises are damaged or destroyed by fire or other casualty or Tenant is evicted from
the Premises by a public authority to preserve the public safety, this Lease shall not terminate, nor shall the liability
of Tenant to pay rent cease or be reduced, but Tenant shall restore, replace or rebuild the Premises at Tenant’s sole
cost and expense with all reasonable speed to the same condition as existed prior to the happening of the fire, eviction
or other casualty. In the event Tenant is required to so restore, replace or rebuild as aforesaid, Tenant shall be entitled
to the proceeds of casualty insurance (if any) payable by virtue of the event or events causing damage to the Premises.
Notwithstanding the foregoing, in the event the Premises are damaged or destroyed within 2 years of the end of the
initial term of this Lease, or any extension thereof rendering the Premises unusable for the intended purpose, and same
cannot be repaired or restored (in Tenant’s reasonable opinion) within six (6) months of the date of such damage or
destruction, Tenant shall have the right to terminate this Lease and all proceeds of insurance attributable to the building
and land shall be delivered to Landlord.
ARTICLE 17. INSURANCE
17.1
Casualty Insurance.
During the entire term of this Lease, Tenant shall maintain the following
insurance regarding the Premises and for the benefit ofLandlord (collectively, the “Insurance”), and shall in all events
name Landlord, and its Lender if any, as an additional insured and/or loss payee, as applicable, as its respective
interests may appear. Tenant shall, at the Tenant’s sole cost and expense, maintain and keep in force and effect fire,
extended coverage and such other casualty insurance as Landlord may specify insurance, including but not limited to
earthquake and flood, with a policy benefit at least equal to the full replacement value of all improvements located on
the Premises. At Landlord’s election, Landlord may obtain such insurance and require Tenant to reimburse Landlord
for all premiums and out-of-pocket costs incurred by Landlord in relation thereto as “Additional Rent.” Tenant shall
maintain insurance on the Premises at an amount equal to 100% of its replacement value. Tenant shall purchase and
13

maintain business interruption insurance to include coverage for a period of interruption of not less than 12 months.
The policy shall provide that: (a) such insurance shall be primary coverage without reduction or right of offset or
contribution on account of any insurance provided by Landlord to itself or its officers, officials or employees; (b) such
insurance shall not be altered or cancelled without 30 days’ written notice to Landlord; such insurance shall name
Landlord as an additional insured; (d) any Fee Mortgagee and Leasehold Mortgagee shall be named as: (i) a loss payee
or mortgagee on Tenant’s property damage insurance policy under a standard mortgagee clause; and (ii) an additional
insured on Tenant’s liability insurance policies. The insurance policies purchased by Tenant must be issued by a
company authorized to conduct business in the State or by a company acceptable to the Landlord.
17.2
General Liability.
In addition, Tenant shall maintain, a Tenant’s sole cost and expense general
public liability insurance against claims for personal injury, death or property damage occurring in, upon or about the
Premises, with coverage of not less than $1,000,000.00 in respect to injury or death of one person, not less than
$2,000,000.00 in respect one any one accident, and not less than $1,000,000.00 in respect to property damage. All
such policies of insurance shall be issued in the name of Tenant with Landlord named as an additional insured for the
mutual and joint benefit and protection of the parties, and certificates evidencing such policies of insurance shall be
delivered to Landlord within 10 days ofbinding or renewal of such policies. Upon Landlord’s request, copies of such
policies shall be delivered to the Landlord. Said policies of Insurance shall provide that Landlord shall be given 30
days prior written notice prior to any modification or cancellation of said policy.
17.3
No Invalidation. Tenant agrees and covenants that it will not do or permit to be done in, to, or about
the Premises any act or thing which will violate, suspend, invalidate or make inoperative any insurance pertaining to
any buildings, other structures or improvements now located in or upon Premises or hereafter constructed and located
thereon or therein; and, further, that Tenant will not permit any buildings, other structures or improvements at any
time to be put, kept or maintained on the property in the condition that the same cannot be insured in the amount of
the full insurable replacement value thereof.
17.4
Delivery of Insurance Certificates.
Upon the commencement of this Lease and at each policy
renewal date, Tenant shall furnish to Landlord, any Fee Mortgagee and any Leasehold Mortgagee insurance
certificates or renewal certificates within 10 days of binding or renewal or, if requested by Landlord, Fee Mortgagee
or Leasehold Mortgagee, certified copies of policies, evidencing all insurance required to be carried by Tenant in
accordance with the Lease. Such certificates or policies shall name Landlord as an insured and shall name any Fee
Mortgagee and Leasehold Mortgagee as mortgagee and loss payee, in accordance with the requirements contained in
this Article 17. The insurance certificate or polices, as applicable, must document that the liability insurance coverage
purchased by the Tenant includes contractual liability coverage to insure the indemnity agreement as stated.
17.5
Evidence of Policy Payments. Tenant shall within 10 days ofpayment furnish to Landlord duplicate
receipts or satisfactory evidence of the payment of all premiums on any and all insurance required to be carried by
Tenant in accordance with this Lease. The insurance carrier shall give Landlord, any Fee Mortgagee and all Leasehold
Mortgagees 10 days’ prior notice (with respect to nonpayment of premiums) of cancellation, modification or non-
renewal.
17.6
Payments for Tenant b Landlord. If Tenant fails to procure the insurance required to be procured
by Tenant under this Lease, or fails to pay any premium of insurance, Impositions, or any other sum in this Lease
required to be paid by Tenant (other than Rent), Landlord may, after expiration of the applicable cure period, at
Landlord’s option, procure on behalf of Tenant any such insurance, and pay on behalf of Tenant any such payment or
payments as may be necessary. Any sum(s) so paid or expended by Landlord on behalf of Tenant shall inimediately
be reimbursed and paid by Tenant to Landlord, as Additional Rent, within ten (7) days after demand by Landlord.
ARTICLE 18. INTENTIONALLY DELETED
ARTICLE 19.
INDEMNIFICATION
19.1
Indemnification of Landlord. Tenant shall defend, indemnify and hold Landlord harmless from and
against any/and all claims, demands, liabilities, and expenses, including reasonable attorneys’ fees, arising from
Tenant’s use of the Premises and Building or from any act permitted, or any omission to act, in or about the Premises
or the Building by Tenant or its agents, employees, contractors, or invitees or from any breach or default by Tenant
14

of this Lease, except to the extent caused solely by Landlord’s gross negligence or intentional misconduct.
In the
event any action or proceeding shall be brought against Landlord by reason of any such claim, Tenant shall defend
the same at Tenant’s expense by counsel reasonably satisfactory to Landlord.
19.2
Indemnification of Tenant. Landlord shall defend, indemnify and hold Tenant harmless from and
against any and all claims, demands, liabilities and expenses, including reasonable attorneys’ fees, arising solely from
Landlord’s operation ofthe Building, or from any act permitted or omission to act, in or about the Building by Landlord
or its agents, employees, contractors, or invitees, or from any breach or default by Landlord of this Lease, except to
the extent caused in whole or in part by Tenant’s gross negligence or intentional misconduct. In the event any action
or proceeding shall be brought against Tenant by reason of any such claim, Landlord shall defend the same at
Landlord’s expense by counsel reasonably satisfactory to Tenant.
ARTICLE 20. ASSIGNMENT, SUBLETTING AND OWNERSHIP
20.1
Tenant. Tenant may not transfer, assign or sublet its interest in this Lease without first obtaining
Landlord’s prior written consent, which shall not be unreasonably withheld or delayed, but which consent may be
conditioned upon Tenant paying for Landlord reasonable costs, including reasonable attorneys’ fees, in reviewing
such assignment or sublease not to exceed $2,500.00. Landlord’s failure to consent shall not be deemed unreasonable
ifproposed assignee or sublessee (i) is not an existing operator of 20 or more quick service restaurants of the franchise
currently operating the Demised Premises; (ii) has less than five years’ experience operating quick service restaurants;
and (iii) does not have a net worth equal to the greater of Tenant’s net worth at the time of the Effective Date of this
Lease, or Tenant’s net worth 45 days prior to the request for assignment or sublessee. Any assignment or subletting
shall not release Tenant from its obligations and liabilities under this Lease. Notwithstanding the foregoing, Tenant
shall have the right to assign this Lease or sublet the Premises or any portion thereof to an Affiliate or a buyer, without
Landlord’s consent, provided that Tenant shall give Landlord prior written notice, which notice shall include the
fmancials of such Affiliate or buyer, thereof and provided such Affiliate or buyer shall have fmancial strength greater
than or equal to Tenant at the time this Lease is entered into by Tenant and Landlord. Affiliate shall mean any entity
that Controls Tenant, any entity that Tenant Controls, or any entity under common Control with Tenant. “Control”
shall mean more than 50% ownership.
20.2
Landlord. Landlord shall have the right to transfer, assign and convey, in whole or in part, any or
all of the right, title and interest to the Building, provided, such transferee or assignee shall be bound by the terms,
covenants and agreements herein contained, and shall expressly assume and agree to perform the covenants and
agreements of Landlord herein contained.
ARTICLE 21. ACCESS TO PREMISES
Upon reasonable prior notice, Landlord or Landlord’s agent shall have the right to enter the Premises during
usual business hours, provided Landlord does not materially interfere with Tenant’s operations to examine the same;
to assist in promotion of the building and Premises of which it is a use of the Premises, to show the Premises to
prospective purchasers or lessees; and to make such repairs, alterations, improvements or additions as Landlord may
deem necessary or desirable, without the same constituting an eviction of Tenant in whole or in part, and the rent
reserved shall in no wise abate, except as otherwise provided below. In the event of an emergency, and if Tenant shall
not be personally present or permit an entry into the Premises, Landlord or Landlord’s agents may forcibly enter the
same without rendering Landlord or such agents liable therefor and without in any manner affecting the obligations
and covenants of this Lease, except Landlord shall be responsible only for damages or injury caused by any
unreasonable forcible entry or negligence. Nothing herein contained, however, shall be deemed or construed to impose
upon Landlord any obligation, responsibility or liability whatever for the care, maintenance or repair of the Premises
or the Building or any part thereof except as otherwise herein specifically provided.
ARTICLE 22. DEFAULTS BY TENANT AND REMEDIES
22.1
Events of Default. The occurrence of any of the following shall constitute a material default and
breach of this Lease by Tenant:
15

a.
Any failure by Tenant to pay Rent within five (5) days of when such is due, or to timely
make any other payment required to be made by Tenant hereunder. In addition, if Tenant fails to make any
payment not made when due or within 5 days thereof, then Tenant shall pay a late payment fee equal to 5%
of the payment then due, plus accruing interest on the amount of the late payment at the rate of 12% per
annum.
b.
A failure by Tenant to observe and perform any material provision of this Lease to be
observed or performed by Tenant, other than a failure to pay Rent, where such failure continues for 10 days
after written notice thereofby Landlord to Tenant; provided, however, if cure thereofreasonably takes longer
than 10 days, this period shall automatically be extended as long as Tenant commences the cure promptly
within said 10 day period and thereafter continues to diligently prosecute completion.
c.
The making by Tenant of any general assigmnent for the benefit of creditors, the filing by
or against Tenant of a petition to have Tenant adjudged a bankrupt, or a petition for reorganization or
arrangement under any law relating to bankruptcy (unless, in the case of a petition filed against Tenant, the
same is dismissed within 60 days the appointment of a trustee or receiver to take possession that is not
restored to Tenant within 30 days, or the attachment, execution or other judicial seizure that is not discharged
within 30 days.
22.2
Remedies.
In the event of any uncured default by Tenant, then Landlord shall be entitled to the
following cumulative remedies (in addition to any other remedies available at law or in equity):
a.
Landlord may terminate this Lease by giving written notice of termination to Tenant, in
which event Tenant shall immediately surrender the Premises to Landlord. If Tenant fails to so surrender the
Premises, then Landlord may, without prejudice to any other remedy it has, including possession of the
Premises or arrearages in Rent or other damages, re-enter and take possession of the Premises and expel or
remove Tenant and any other person occupying the Premises or any part thereof, in accordance with
applicable law; or
b.
Landlord may re-enter and take possession of the Premises without terminating this Lease
and relet the Premises and apply the Rent received to the account of Tenant. If Landlord so re-enters and
takes possession of the Premises as set forth above, Landlord agrees to use reasonable efforts to relet the
Premises for a commercially reasonable rate at the time of such reletting. No reletting by Landlord shall be
considered to be for Landlord’s own account, unless Landlord has notified Tenant in writing that this Lease
has been terminated. In addition, no such reletting shall be considered an acceptance of Tenant’s surrender
of the Premises unless Landlord so notifies Tenant in writing; or
c.
Landlord may re-enter the Premises without terminating this Lease and without being liable
for any damages, unless caused by the negligence or misconduct of Landlord, and do whatever Tenant is
obligated to do under this Lease. Tenant shall pay to Landlord, upon demand, the reasonable expenses paid
by Landlord in satisfying Tenant’s obligations under the terms of this Lease.
Any sums so expended by
Landlord shall bear interest at the rate of 18% per annum from the date expended until the date Landlord is
repaid.
22.3
Abandonment. If Landlord obtains possession of the Premises as a result of Tenant’s abandonment
of same or by a decree from a court of competent jurisdiction, this shall not be construed as an election to terminate
this Lease unless Landlord provides Tenant with a written notice of this election
ARTICLE 23. DEFAULTS BY LANDLORD
If Landlord should be in default in the performance of any of its obligations under this Lease, which default
continues for a period of more than 15 days after receipt of written notice from Tenant specifying such default, or if
such default is of a nature to require more than 15 days for remedy and continues beyond the time reasonably necessary
to cure (and Landlord has not undertaken procedures to cure the default within such 15 day period and diligently
pursued such efforts to complete such cure), Tenant may, in addition to any other remedy available at law or in equity
16

at its option, incur any reasonable expense necessary to perform the obligation of Landlord specified in such notice
and deduct such expense from the Rent or other charges next becoming due.
ARTICLE 24. SURRENDER OF PREMISES
Tenant shall, upon the expiration of the Term granted herein, or any earlier termination of this Lease for any
cause, surrender to Landlord the Premises, including, without limitation, all building apparatus and equipment then
upon the Premises, and all alterations, improvements and other additions which may be made or installed by either
party to, in, upon or about the Premises. Tenant may not remove its improvements to or permanent alterations of the
Premises, but it may remove Tenant’s property, as defmed in Article 10 above.
ARTICLE 25. EMINENT DOMAIN
25.1
Termination of Lease. In the event a substantial portion of the Building or parking shall be
appropriated or taken under the power of eminent domain by any public or quasi-public authority, then at the election
of Landlord or Tenant upon 10 days written notice, this Lease shall terminate and expire as of the date of such taking,
and both Landlord and Tenant shall thereupon be released from any liability thereafter accruing hereunder. A
“substantial portion of the Building” means 20% or more of the rentable square feet of the Building.
25.2
Election Not to Terminate. If the Lease is not terminated pursuant to Article 25.1 above, Tenant
shall remain in that portion of the Premises, which shall not have been appropriated or taken, and all Rent and payment
obligations ofTenant shall be adjusted on an equitable basis, taking into account the relative value of the portion taken
as compared to the portion remaining. For the purpose of this Article 25.2, a voluntary sale or conveyance in lieu of
condemnation, but under threat of condemnation shall be deemed an appropriation or taking under the power of
eminent domain.
25.3
Landlord’s Right to Compensation. All compensation awarded or paid for the total or partial
taking of the Premises through the exercise of the power of eminent domain shall belong to Landlord without
participation by Tenant and without any deduction therefrom for any present or future estate of Tenant. No claim of
Tenant made pursuant to Article 25.4 below shall diminish or otherwise adversely affect Landlord’s award.
25.4
Tenant’s Right to Compensation. Nothing herein shall be deemed to affect Tenant’s right to
receive compensation from the entity exercising its power of eminent domain for damages to Tenant’s Property only.
ARTICLE 26. ATTORNEYS’ FEES
In the event of any default under the terms of this Lease or any dispute regarding its terms and conditions or
enforcement, the defaulting party agrees to reimburse the prevailing party for all expenses and costs, including
reasonable attorneys’ fees incurred in enforcing the terms hereof. Such reimbursement shall include all legal expenses
incurred, whether enforcing is sought by suit or otherwise.
ARTICLE 27. NOTICES
Notices and demands required, or permitted, to be sent to those listed hereunder shall be sent by certified
mail, return receipt requested, postage prepaid, or by Federal Express or other reputable overnight courier service and
shall be deemed to have been given upon the date the same is postmarked, ifsent by certified mail, or the day deposited
with Federal Express or such other reputable overnight courier service:
Landlord:
Tenant:
RCB North 1, LLC
HRARG DH CHEYENNE WY, LLC
5284 S Commerce Drive, Suite C-274
2999 N. 44th Street, Suite 400
Murray, Utah 84117
Phoenix, Arizona 85018
or at such other address requested in writing by either party upon thirty (30) days’ notice to the other party.
17

ARTICLE 28. REMEDIES
All rights and remedies of Landlord and Tenant herein created or otherwise extending at law are cumulative,
and the exercise of one or more rights or remedies may be exercised and enforced concurrently or consecutively and
whenever and as often as deemed desirable.
ARTICLE 29. SUCCESSORS AND ASSIGNS
All covenants, promises, conditions, representations and agreements herein contained shall be binding upon,
apply and inure to the parties hereto and their respective heirs, executors, administrators, successors and assigns; it
being understood and agreed, however, that the provisions of Article 20 are in nowise impaired by this Article 29.
ARTICLE 30. WAIVER
The failure of Landlord or Tenant to insist upon strict performance by the other of any of the covenants,
conditions, and agreements of this Lease shall not be deemed a waiver of any subsequent breach or default by the
other in any of the covenants, conditions and agreements of this Lease. No surrender of the Premises by Tenant shall
be affected by Landlord’s acceptance of Rent or by other means whatsoever unless the same is evidenced by
Landlord’s written acceptance of the surrender.
ARTICLE 31. HOLDING OVER
If Tenant or any party claiming under Tenant remains in possession of the Premises or any part thereof after
any termination or expiration of this Lease, Landlord, in Landlord’s sole discretion, may treat such holdover as an
automatic renewal of this Lease for a four-week tenancy subject to all the terms and conditions provided herein,
provided, however that, the Base Rent during the holdover may not exceed the then-current base rental by more than
150%.
ARTICLE 32. INTERPRETATION
The parties hereto agree that it is their intention hereby to create only the relationship of Landlord and Tenant,
and no provision hereof, or act of either party hereunder, shall ever be construed as creating the relationship of
principal and agent, or a partnership, or a joint venture or enterprise between the parties hereto.
ARTICLE 33. COVENANT OF TITLE AND QUIET ENJOYMENT
Landlord covenants that Tenant or any permitted assignee or sublessee of Tenant, upon the payment of the
Rent and performance of the covenants hereunder, shall and may peaceably and quietly have, hold and enjoy the
Premises and improvements thereon during the Term or any renewal or extension thereof. Additionally, Landlord
shall take no action that will interfere with Tenant’s intended usage of the Premises.
ARTICLE 34. RECORDING
Tenant shall not record this Lease, but may record a memorandum of this Lease in a form acceptable to
Landlord with the Laramie County Recorder. Any recording costs associated with the memorandum shall be borne
by Tenant.
ARTICLE 35. FORCE MAJEURE AND PERMITTED DELAYS
In the event that either party hereto shall be delayed or hindered in or prevented from the performance
required hereunder by reason of strikes, lockouts, labor troubles, failure ofpower, riots, insurrection, war, acts of God,
or other reason of like nature not the fault of the party delayed in performing work or doing acts (hereinafter,
“Permitted Delay” or “Permitted Delays”), such party shall be excused for the period of time equivalent to the delay
caused by such Permitted Delay. Notwithstanding the foregoing, any extension of time for a Permitted Delay shall be
conditioned upon the party seeking an extension of time delivering written notice of such Permitted Delay to the other
party within 10 days of the event causing the Permitted Delay, and the maximum period of time which Landlord may
18

delay any act or performance of work due to a Permitted Delay shall be 60 days. Notwithstanding the preceding,
nothing in this Article shall excuse any obligations of Tenant involving the payment of money.
ARTICLE 36. LIMITATIONS ON LANDLORD’S LIABILITY
Notwithstanding anything to the contrary contained in this Lease, in the event of any default or breach by
Landlord with respect to any ofthe terms, covenants and conditions of this Lease to be observed, honored or performed
by Landlord, Tenant shall look solely to the estate and property of Landlord in the land and building(s) owned by
Landlord comprising the Premises for the collection of any judgment (or any other judicial procedures requiring the
payment of money by Landlord) and no other property or assets of Landlord shall be subject to levy, execution, or
other procedures for satisfaction of Tenant’s remedies.
ARTICLE 37. SEVERABILITY
Any provision of this Lease which shall prove to be invalid, void or illegal shall in no way affect, impair or
invalidate any other provisions hereof and such other provisions shall remain in full force and effect.
ARTICLE 38. GOVERNING LAW AND VENUE
This Lease shall be governed by the laws of the State of Wyoming.
Tenant and Landlord agree that any
claims related to this Lease shall be filed in the State courts located in Wyoming or Utah.
ARTICLE 39. BROKERS
Each of Landlord and Tenant represent and warrant that it has not retained a real estate broker or other broker
in connection with this Lease. Landlord and Tenant indemnify and hold each other harmless from and against any
and all liability and cost which Landlord or Tenant may suffer in connection with real estate brokers claiming by,
through, or under either party seeking any commission, fee or payment in connection with this Lease.
ARTICLE 40. TENANT’S CONDUCT OF BUSINESS
Tenant agrees, at such time it is operating its business in the Premises, to conduct its business in a first-class
manner consistent with reputable business standards and practices.
ARTICLE 41. OBLIGATIONS THAT SURVWE EXPIRATION OF THIS LEASE
In addition to those obligations specifically referred to in this Lease, as surviving the expiration or termination
of this Lease, the following obligations shall survive the expiration or termination of this Lease: (a) any obligation
herein permitted to be performed after the end of the termination of this Lease; (b) any obligation not reasonably
susceptible of performance prior to the termination of this Lease; (c) any obligation arising under this Agreement to
indemnify, defend or hold any person harmless, and (d) any obligation to be performed pursuant hereto at or before
the end of the Term, which is not so performed.
ARTICLE 42. RULES AND REGULATIONS; DECLARATION
Tenant shall faithfully observe and comply with (i) the reasonable rules and regulations that Landlord shall,
from time to time, promulgate and/or modify and (ii) with any Declaration or CC&Rs filed with the County Recorder
relating to the Premises. The rules and regulations shall be binding upon the Tenant upon delivery of a copy of them
to Tenant. Landlord shall not be responsible to Tenant for the non-performance of the above by any other tenants or
occupants of the Building.
ARTICLE 43. ESTOPPEL CERTIFICATE
Landlord or Tenant shall, within ten (10) days after request by the other party, (i) execute and deliver to a
written declaration (to the extent true) in substantially the same form as that attached hereto as Exhibit E: (a) ratifying
this Lease; (b) expressing the commencement and termination dates thereof and whether any renewal options have
19

been exercised; (c) certifying that this Lease is in full force and effect and has not been assigned, modified,
supplemented or amended (except by such writings as shall be stated); (d) that all conditions under this Lease to be
performed by the party have been satisfied; (e) that there are no defenses or offsets against the enforcement of this
Lease by Landlord, or stating those claimed by Tenant; (f) the amount of advance rental, if any, (or none if such is the
case) paid by Tenant; (g) the date to which rental has been paid; (h) the amount of security deposited with Landlord,
if any; and (i) such other reasonable factual statements as shall be requested. Such declaration shall be executed and
delivered from time to time as may be requested by Landlord or Tenant.
ARTICLE 44. LEASE ADDENDUM - DOG HAUS
Landlord and Tenant acknowledge and agree that Tenant shall use the Premises as a Dog Haus
Restaurant, pursuant to a franchise agreement (“Franchise Agreement”) between Tenant and Dog Haus International,
LLC (“Dog Haus”). Simultaneous with the execution of this Lease, Landlord and Tenant shall enter into an Addendum
to Lease substantially in the form attached hereto as Exhibit G, which Addendum contains additional terms related to
Dog Haus.
ARTICLE 45. ENTIRE AGREEMENT
This Lease contains all of the agreements of the parties hereto with respect to matters covered or mentioned
in this Lease and no prior agreement, letters, representations, warranties, promises, or understandings pertaining to
any such matters shall be effective for any such purpose.
This Lease may be amended or added to only by an
agreement in writing signed by the parties hereto or their respective successors in interest.
ARTICLE 46. ACCORD AND SATISFACTION
No payment by Tenant or receipt by Landlord of a lesser amount than the required Rent herein stipulated
shall be deemed to be other than on account of the earliest stipulated rent nor shall any endorsement or statement on
any check or any letter accompanying any check or payment as Rent be deemed an accord and satisfaction; and
Landlord may accept such check or payment without prejudice to Landlord’s right to recover the balance of any Rent
or to pursue any other remedy in this Lease provided.
ARTICLE 47. MINIMUM STORE REQUIREMENT
Tenant acknowledges that Landlord is relying upon Tenant’s representations regarding Tenant’s current
business plan to open and operate at least twenty (20) stores during the Term of this Lease, and during the Term of
this Lease, Landlord shall provide to Tenant, subject to customary confidentiality obligations, information regarding
the number of Tenant’s other business locations and Tenant’s fmancial condition. Tenant hereby covenants and agrees
that once Tenant achieves twenty (20) stores during the Term of this Lease in accordance with Tenant’s current
business plan, Tenant, and or the Guarantors, collectively, will at all times thereafter during the Term of this Lease
and including any Renewal Term have at least twenty (20) stores current and open and operating.
ARTICLE 48. USURY
Any amounts payable by Tenant hereunder shall not exceed the maximum non-usurious amount that may be
charged under applicable law.
20

IN WITNESS WHEREOF, the parties hereto have executed this Lease for all purposes as
of the date and year first written above.

EXHIBIT G
LEASE ADDENDUM- DOG HAUS

ADDENDUISI TO LEASE AGREEMENT
THIS ADDENDUM TO LEASE AGREEMENT (this “Addendum”) is effective as of
_________________
2017 (the “Effective Date”). and is being signed simultaneously with the Lease
(the “Lease”) dated
________________________.
2017 between
__________________________
(the “Franchisee” or “Tenant”) and
_______________________
(the “Landlord”) for the real property
conunonly known as
_______________________________________
(the “Premises”).
1.
hicoiyoration and Precedence. This Addendum is incorporated into the Lease and
supersedes any conflicting provisions in it. Capitalized terms not otherwise defined in this
Addendum have the meanings as defmed in the Lease.
2.
Backound. The Tenant will operate a Dog Haus restaurant at the Premises
under a Franchise Agreement (the “Franchise Agreement”) with Dog Haus International. LLC
(the ‘Franchisor”). By entering into a franchise relationship with the Franchisor. the Tenant has
agreed to grant the Franchisor a seculity interest in the Lease, all of the furniture, fixtures,
inventory and supplies located in the Premises as collateral for the payment of any obligation.
liability or other amount owed by the Tenant or its affiliates to the Franchisor under the
Franchise Agreement. The Franchise Agreement also requires that the Lease contain certain
provisions that the Tenant is requesting the Landlord to include.
3.
Marks. The Tenant has the right to display the trade and service marks of the
Franchisor in accordance with the specifications required by the Franchisor. subject only to the
provisions of applicable law, for the term of the Lease.
4.
Easement. The Landlord grants to the Tenant during the term of the Lease a non-
exclusive iight and easement over that portion of the property as may be required by the Tenant
to iniprove. renovate, repair. replace and maintain the Premises or replace its signage or its panel
on the pylon sign for the property. The Tenant has the right to change or alter the signage at any
time during the terni of the Lease provided the signage is in compliance with all applicable
governmental codes and regulations. The signage may include: (a) signage on the exterior front
wall of the Premises: (b) signage on another exterior portion of the Premises; (c) a separate pylon
sign on the property: (d) separate signage on the property. (e) a panel on the pylon sign for the
property: and (f) other signage which may be required by the Franchisor or agreed upon by the
Landlord and the Tenant.
5.
Copies of Reports. The Landlord agrees to provide copies of all revenue and
other information and data in Landlord’s possession related to the operation of the Tenant’s Dog
Haus restaurant on a timely basis as the Franchisor may request. during the tenli of the Lease.
6.
Notice of Default. The Landlord will give written notice to the Franchisor
(concurrently with the giving of such notice to the Tenant) of any defaults (a “Default”) by the
Tenant under the Lease by certified mail, return receipt requested. or by nationally recognized
overnight courier service, at the following address or to such other address as the Franchisor may
provide to Landlord from time to time:

Dog Haus International. LLC
22 Central Court
Pasadena. California 91105
Attention: Franchise Administrator
Such notice vill grant the Franchisor the right, but not the obligation, to cure any Default, if the
Tenant fails to do so, within an additional fifteen (15) days after the expiration of the period in
which the Tenant may cure the Default under the Lease.
7.
Franchisor’s Assumption of Lease. In the event of a default of the Lease by
Tenant or the default of the Franchise Agreement by Tenant. and upon written notice by the
Franchisor to have the Lease assigned to the Franchisor or a qualified franchisee of Franchisor as
lessee (the “Assigmnent Notice”). (i) the Franchisor or a qualified franchisee of Franchisor will
become the lessee of the Premises and will be liable for all obligations under the Lease arising
after the date of the Assignment Notice and (ii) the Landlord will recognize the Franchisor or a
qualified franchisee of Franchisor as the lessee of the Premises effective as of the date of the
Assignment Notice. Notwithstanding anything in the Lease to the contrary. Landlord agrees that
the lease and the right, title and interest of Tenant and any subsequent or successor Tenant
thereunder. may be assigned to Franchisor or its parents. subsidiaries or affiliates or to a
qualified franchisee of Franchisor. In the event of an assignment to a Franchisor entity,
Franchisor shall at all times have the right to reassign the Lease. without charge and without
Landlord consent being required to a qualified franchisee of Franchisor and the Franchisor entity
shall thereupon be released from any further liability under the Lease.
8.
Default Under Franchise Agreement. Any Default under the Lease which is not
cured by Tenant within any applicable cure period also constitutes grounds for tennination of the
Franchise Agreement.
9.
Use and Competing Business. Tenant shall only use the Premises for the purpose
of operating a “Dog Haus” restaurant that engages in the sale of hot dogs. sausages. burgers and
other related food products and side dishes for dine-in or take-out. including any incidental
merchandising as is customary in a Dog Haus restaurant now or in the future. Landlord shall not
during the term of the Lease, including any renewals or extensions. permit directly or indirectly.
another restaurant in the shopping center with more than 6 beers on tap or that sells hot dogs.
sausages, or burgers for dine-in or take-out. In the event that Landlord breaches this provision.
then Tenant shall be entitled to an inunediate reduction of its rent to One Dollar ($1.00) per month
until such time as the breach is cured if possible or for the remainder of the tenn of the Lease and
any renewals or extensions thereof if the breach cannot be cured.
10.
Non-Disturbance. So long as the Lease term continues and the Tenant is not in
Default under the Lease, the Tenant’s use. possession and enjoyment of the Premises will not be
interfered with by any lender of the Landlord or any other person. The Landlord agrees to use its
best efforts to obtain prior to conunencement of the Lease any documents necessary to ensure the
foregoing. including a Subordination. Non-Disturbance and Attoriunent Agreement or similar
agreement.

11.
Latent Defects. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Lease. Landlord will
repair any latent defects to the Premises discovered by Tenant and communicate in writing to
Landlord within one (1) year of execution of the Lease.
12.
Amendment. The Landlord and the Tenant will not cancel, terminate, modify or
amend the Lease including, without limitation. the Franchisor’s rights under this Addendum,
without the Franchisor’s prior written consent.
13.
Benefits and Successors. The benefits of this Addendum inure to the Franchisor
and to its successor and assigns.
14.
Remaining Provisions Unaffected. Those parts of the Lease that are not expressly
modified by this Addendum remain in full force and effect.
Intending to be bound. the Landlord and the Tenant sign and deliver this Addendum
effective on the Effective Date, regardless of the actual date of signature.
Landlord
Tenant
